### Main Turkish event chain

add_namespace = Turkey

####################### Turkey Submod ###########################

##Event: Operation Of Return To Life
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.1
 is_triggered_only = yes
 title = Turkey.1.title
 desc = Turkey.1.desc
 picture = GFX_hayat
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 }
         AND = {
             date > 2000.1.1
             date < 2000.2.1
         }
    }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_1 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

	add_political_power = -15
	}
}

############################################# 2001 ##############################




##Event: Turkish Economic Crisis 2001
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.3
 is_triggered_only = yes
 title = Turkey.3.title
 desc = Turkey.3.desc
 picture = GFX_faiz
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		 NOT = {
		 has_idea = reformed_2001_crisis
		 has_idea = reforms_for_2001_crisis
		 has_idea = reformss_for_2001_crisis
		 }
		 #has_idea = pr_necdet
		 #western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 }
         AND = {
             date > 2001.2.20
             date < 2002.3.20
         }
    }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_3 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.3.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

			custom_effect_tooltip = UNION_DECREASE_10
		subtract_from_variable = { TUR_union_acceptance = 10 }
	add_ideas = turkish_2001_crisis
swap_ideas = { 
  remove_idea = turkish_IMF_reformation_1
  add_idea = turkish_IMF_reformation_2
}
			hidden_effect = { country_event = Turkey_news.7 } 
	add_ideas = depression
	set_variable = { TUR_interest_rate = 7500 }
	set_variable = { TUR_amount_lira_dollar = 666774 }
	set_variable = { TUR_lira_amount_against_euro = 1143276}
	set_variable = { TUR_lira_amount_against_sterlin = 1645478 }
	}
}

			}
	}

##Event: Elections Campaign 
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.11
 title = Turkey.11.title
 desc = Turkey.11.desc
 picture = GFX_election_day
 is_triggered_only = yes

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = Turkey.11.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

    country_event = MD4_election_campaign.0
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = democratic
				last_election = "2002.11.3" #to give correct future elections
					election_frequency = 60
					elections_allowed = yes
				}
	add_stability = 0.02
    ### Grand National Assembly
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_akp value = 363 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_chp value = 178 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_dp value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_hdp value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_mhp value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_tkp value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_good value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_green value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_independent value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_empty value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_total value = 550 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_fp value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_par_anap value = 0 }
	}
}

##Event: Anti American Protests For American Plan For Invading Iraq
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.12
 is_triggered_only = yes
 title = Turkey.12.title
 desc = Turkey.12.desc
 picture = GFX_politics_protest
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		 USA = { has_war_with = IRQ }
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 } 
    }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_12 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.12.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

	add_war_support = -0.05
		clr_country_flag = besimtibuk

	}
}

##Event: America Wants Us To Join Iraq War
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.13
 is_triggered_only = yes
 title = Turkey.13.title
 desc = Turkey.13.desc
 picture = GFX_turkeys_afghan_operation
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		 USA = { has_war_with = IRQ }
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 } 
    }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_13 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.13.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

	add_political_power = 75
	USA = { country_event = Turkey.14 }
	}
	option = {
		name = Turkey.13.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

		create_wargoal = {
			type = annex_everything
			target = IRQ
		}
		transfer_state = 633
		IRQ = {
        add_timed_idea = { idea = TUR_iraqi_defense days = 50}
		}
			 add_political_power = -50
			 add_stability = -0.07
			subtract_from_variable = { treasury = -5.25 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = 5_25_billion_revenue_tt
			USA = { 
			subtract_from_variable = { treasury = 6 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = 6_billion_expense_tt
			}
	}

	option = {
		name = Turkey.13.c
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

	add_stability = 0.07
	} 
}

    limit = {

	}

		name = Turkey.120.b

	}

		name = Turkey.121.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

	remove_unit_leader_role = { character = mustafa_ozsoy }

 title = Turkey.153.title
 desc = Turkey.153.desc
 picture = GFX_istanbul_fight
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
				 has_elections = yes
neutrality_neutral_muslim_brotherhood_are_in_power = yes
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 } 
 NOT = { has_completed_focus = CHP_a_new_start_for_turkey}
         AND = {
             date > 2019.3.31
	         date < 2019.4.31
         }
    }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_153 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.153.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 0}

	add_stability = -0.02
	add_political_power = -75
    custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_CHP_increase
		hidden_effect = { sound_effect = "speechev_ekrem_imamoglu_victory" }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TR_speechev_ekrem_imamoglu_victory
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^3 = 0.05 }
				recalculate_party = yes
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = neutrality
				popularity = -0.025
			}
	}


	option = {
		name = Turkey.153.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

	add_stability = -0.02
	add_political_power = -75
    custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_CHP_increase

country_event = {
 id = Turkey.253
 is_triggered_only = yes
 title = Turkey.253.title
 desc = Turkey.253.desc
 picture = GFX_ince_speech
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		 	neutrality_neutral_muslim_brotherhood_are_in_power = yes
		  AND = {
             date > 2018.2.1
             date < 2018.3.1
         }
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 } 
    }


	immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_253 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.253.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

	if = {
    limit = {
    NOT = { has_global_flag = BLOCKED_SOUNDS } }
			hidden_effect = { sound_effect = "speechev_muharrem_ince_speech" }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TR_speechev_muharrem_ince_speech
	}
            add_political_power = -25
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^3 = 0.03 }
				recalculate_party = yes
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_CHP_increase
		}	
	option = {
		name = Turkey.253.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 0}

	if = {
    limit = {
    NOT = { has_global_flag = BLOCKED_SOUNDS } }
			hidden_effect = { sound_effect = "speechev_muharrem_ince_speech" }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TR_speechev_muharrem_ince_speech
	}

##Event: MHP Reaction To Military
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.293
 is_triggered_only = yes
 title = Turkey.293.title
 desc = Turkey.293.desc
 picture = GFX_coup_tanks
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
         has_completed_focus = TUR_left_armed_forces
         nationalist_right_wing_populists_are_in_power = yes		 
    }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_293 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.293.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

swap_ideas = { 
  remove_idea = turkish_military_infulence
  add_idea = turkish_military_infulence_coup
}

	set_variable = { ruling_party = 4 }

		hidden_effect = {
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			set_variable = { ruling_party = max_index_1 }
		}

		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				set_country_flag = [META_SET_PARTY1]
			}
			META_SET_PARTY1 = "[meta_set_ideology1]"
		}

		set_leader = yes #set in desicion which party is selected
		update_government_coalition_strength = yes
		clear_coalition_count_ideas = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^22 = 1.0 }
				recalculate_party = yes
			}

			create_country_leader = {
				name = "İlker Başbuğ"
				picture = "ktu.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
					military_career
				}
			}

						add_popularity = {
							ideology = nationalist
							popularity = 0.45
						}	
                set_politics = {
                ruling_party = nationalist
                elections_allowed = no
            }	
	}	
  }

	    add_ideas = turkic_state

	AZE = {
    add_ideas = turkic_state
			}

    add_ideas = turkic_state
			} 
	KAZ = {
    add_ideas = turkic_state

			}

 title = Turkey.386.title
 desc = Turkey.386.desc
 picture = GFX_creation_of_the_turkic_union
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		  AND = {
	      date > 2023.3.1
	      date < 2023.4.1
         }
		 NOT = {
		 has_global_flag = TUR_turkic_unification
		 }
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 } 
    }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_386 }
	option = {
		name = Turkey.386.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

     custom_effect_tooltip = UKRAINE_AND_FINLAND_JOINS_TURKIC_UNION
     FIN = {
	 set_country_flag = TURKIC_spectator
}	 
	 UKR = {
	 set_country_flag = TURKIC_spectator
	}	
  } }

             date > 2017.11.10

         }

         }

	set_variable = { TUR_lira_amount_against_bitcoin = 30128 }

 title = Turkey.734.title
 desc = Turkey.734.desc
 picture = GFX_thy_buys_boeing
 fire_only_once = yes

 trigger = {
  tag = TUR
  AND = {
   date > 2026.1.1
   date < 2026.2.1
  }
  NOT = {
   has_government = fascism
   has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
   has_global_flag = ERDOGAN_NOT_ACHIEVED
   has_capitulated = yes
  }
 }


 immediate = { set_country_flag = TUR_evf_Turkey_734 }
 option = {
  name = Turkey.734.a
  ai_chance = {
   factor = 100
  }
  add_political_power = 30
			subtract_from_variable = { treasury = 0.025 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = 25_million_expense_tt
 }
}

 picture = GFX_zafer_talks
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		  AND = {
             date > 2023.3.11

         }
neutrality_neutral_muslim_brotherhood_are_in_power = yes
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
         has_global_flag = ERDOGAN_NOT_ACHIEVED
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 }

		}

	}
